Clean. All type errors resolved. Summary of changes:

1. **`opensearch-neural.service.ts`**:
   - `brand: style.brand` → `brand: style.brand ? [style.brand] : null` (wrap string→array for OpenSearch doc)
   - `buildCombinedTextForEmbedding`: `style.brand?.length` / `style.brand[0]` → `style.brand` (direct string)

2. **`process-style.service.ts`**:
   - `this.getBrand([style.brand])` → `this.getBrand({ array: [style.brand] })` (wrong call signature)

3. **Test fixtures** — `brand` changed from `string[]` to `string | null`:
   - `opensearch-neural.service.e2e-spec.ts`: `['TestBrand']` → `'TestBrand'`, `['Nike', 'Adidas']` → `'Nike'`, `[]` → `null`
   - `opensearch-test-helper.ts`: `['Brand 1']` → `'Brand 1'`
   - `product-hydration.service.e2e-spec.ts`: `['Nike', 'Adidas']` → `'Nike'`
   - `trending-products.service.e2e-spec.ts`: added missing `embedding_text: null, material: null` to two fixtures

Perfect! Now I can provide a comprehensive analysis of the failing tests and coverage gaps.

## Comprehensive Analysis of Failing Tests and Coverage Gaps

### Summary of Findings

There are **7 failing tests** out of 1496 tests. All failures are in the `NeuralSearchService (e2e)` test suite and stem from a **data mismatch between test expectations and the actual configuration constant**.

---

## **CRITICAL ISSUE: Hardcoded Test Data vs. Real Configuration**

The tests in `/test/rest/neural-search.service.e2e-spec.ts` hardcode expected category data that no longer matches the actual `NEURAL_SEARCH_TAG_CATEGORY_NAMES` constant.

### **File: `src/constants/neural-search.constant.ts` (Lines 1-55)**

The actual configuration includes query text descriptions for categories:

**Events use-case (actual):**
```typescript
Events: [
    { 'T-Shirts': '' },
    { 'Tote Bags': 'Midweight Cotton tote bag' },      // ← Has description
    { Drinkware: '' },
    { Sunglasses: '' },
]
```

**Trade Shows use-case (actual):**
```typescript
'Trade Shows': [
    { 'Business Cards': 'Horizontal business card' },
    { 'Tote Bags': 'Midweight Cotton tote bag' },
    { Sunglasses: 'Solid Promotional Sunglasses' },
    { 'Lanyards & Badge Holders': 'Lanyard with Lobster Clip' },
    { Signs: 'color banner kit' },                      // ← 6 categories total
    { Tablecloths: 'Full color tablecloths' },
],
```

---

## **Failing Tests Details**

### **Test 1: "should forward limit parameter as size" (Line 409-425)**

**File:** `/test/rest/neural-search.service.e2e-spec.ts`

**Location:** Lines 409-425

**Expected:**
```typescript
expect(opensearchNeuralService.searchProductsByCategories).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
    categories: [
        { 'T-Shirts': '' },
        { 'Tote Bags': '' },           // ← Expects empty string
        { Drinkware: '' },
        { Sunglasses: '' },
    ],
    size: 10,
});
```

**Received:**
```typescript
Object {
    "categories": Array [
        Object {
            "T-Shirts": "",
        },
        Object {
            "Tote Bags": "Midweight Cotton tote bag",  // ← Has description!
        },
        Object {
            "Drinkware": "",
        },
        Object {
            "Sunglasses": "",
        },
    ],
}
```

**Root Cause:** The test expects `'Tote Bags': ''` but the constant has `'Tote Bags': 'Midweight Cotton tote bag'`

---

### **Test 2: "should work with use-case names containing spaces" (Line 467-483)**

**File:** `/test/rest/neural-search.service.e2e-spec.ts`

**Location:** Lines 467-483

**Expected:**
```typescript
expect(opensearchNeuralService.searchProductsByCategories).toHaveBeenCalledWith({
    categories: [
        { 'Promotional Products': '' },
        { 'Tote Bags': '' },
        { Sunglasses: '' },
        { 'Trade Show Signage': '' },
    ],
});
```

**Received:**
```typescript
Object {
    "categories": Array [
        Object {
            "Business Cards": "Horizontal business card",      // ✗ Wrong category
        },
        Object {
            "Tote Bags": "Midweight Cotton tote bag",
        },
        Object {
            "Sunglasses": "Solid Promotional Sunglasses",
        },
        Object {
            "Lanyards & Badge Holders": "Lanyard with Lobster Clip",
        },
        Object {
            "Signs": "color banner kit",                       // ✗ Extra categories
        },
        Object {
            "Tablecloths": "Full color tablecloths",
        },
    ],
}
```

**Root Cause:** 
- Test expects 4 categories; actual constant has 6 categories for 'Trade Shows'
- Test expects `'Promotional Products'` and `'Trade Show Signage'`; actual constant has `'Business Cards'`, `'Lanyards & Badge Holders'`, `'Signs'`, and `'Tablecloths'`
- Test expects empty descriptions; actual constant has query text descriptions

---

## **Source Files Analysis**

### **1. `src/modules/opensearch/opensearch-neural.service.ts`**

**The `searchProducts` method (Lines 458-607):**
- **Key field check:** `this.getModelId()` is called at line 577
- **Issue:** `getModelId()` (lines 93-100) throws an error if `this.modelId` is null
- The method properly checks model availability before attempting neural search

**The `onModuleInit` method (Lines 39-46):**
```typescript
async onModuleInit(): Promise<void> {
    this.indexName = this.configService.get<string>({ key: OPENSEARCH_INDEX_NAME });
    await this.resolveModelId();
    
    if (!this.modelId) {
        await this.attemptAutoRedeploy();
    }
}
```

**The `modelId` field (Line 32):**
```typescript
private modelId: string | null = null;
```
- Initialized as null
- Updated via `resolveModelId()` (lines 53-71)
- Protected by `getModelId()` getter (lines 93-100)

---

### **2. `src/modules/rest/neural-search/neural-search.service.ts`**

**The `getProductsByUseCase` method (Lines 52-78):**
```typescript
async getProductsByUseCase({
    useCase,
    limit,
}: {
    useCase: string;
    limit?: number;
}): Promise<NeuralSearchUseCaseResponseDto> {
    const categories = NEURAL_SEARCH_TAG_CATEGORY_NAMES[useCase];
    
    if (!categories) {
        const validUseCases = Object.keys(NEURAL_SEARCH_TAG_CATEGORY_NAMES).join(', ');
        throw new BadRequestException(
            `Invalid use-case "${useCase}". Valid options: ${validUseCases}`,
        );
    }
    
    const products = await this.opensearchNeuralService.searchProductsByCategories({
        categories,
        ...(limit !== undefined && { size: limit }),
    });
    
    return {
        useCase,
        total: products.length,
        products,
    };
}
```

**Critical Issue:** The method forwards the `categories` directly from the constant. The test expectations are **hardcoded to old data**.

---

## **Coverage Gaps Summary**

| Test Name | File | Lines | Status | Issue |
|-----------|------|-------|--------|-------|
| should throw error when model ID is not available | opensearch-neural.service.e2e-spec.ts | 322-331 | ✅ PASS | Properly mocks null modelId |
| should search successfully when model is available | opensearch-neural.service.e2e-spec.ts | 355-387 | ✅ PASS | Uses mocked opensearch search |
| should limit results to requested size | opensearch-neural.service.e2e-spec.ts | 497-514 | ✅ PASS | Returns correctly limited array |
| should forward limit parameter as size | neural-search.service.e2e-spec.ts | 409-425 | ❌ FAIL | Expects `{ 'Tote Bags': '' }` but gets `{ 'Tote Bags': 'Midweight Cotton tote bag' }` |
| should work with use-case names containing spaces | neural-search.service.e2e-spec.ts | 467-483 | ❌ FAIL | Expects 4 categories but gets 6; wrong category names |

---

## **The Real Problem**

The `NeuralSearchService` implementation is **correct** — it properly forwards the `limit` as `size` and correctly looks up categories from the constant. However:

1. **Test expectations are outdated** — they were written before the constant was updated with query text descriptions
2. **The constant was changed** but tests were not synchronized
3. **No coverage for actual constant behavior** — tests need to match the real `NEURAL_SEARCH_TAG_CATEGORY_NAMES` data

All 7 failures are in the same test file and root cause is data mismatch, not code bugs.

28	@Injectable()
29	export class OpensearchNeuralService implements OnModuleInit {
30		private readonly logger = new Logger(OpensearchNeuralService.name);
31		private indexName: string;
32		private modelId: string | null = null;
33	
34		constructor(
35			private readonly opensearchService: OpensearchService,
36			private readonly configService: EnvConfigService,
37		) {}
38	
39		async onModuleInit(): Promise<void> {
40			this.indexName = this.configService.get<string>({ key: OPENSEARCH_INDEX_NAME });
41			await this.resolveModelId();
42	
43			if (!this.modelId) {
44				await this.attemptAutoRedeploy();
45			}
46		}
47	
48		@Interval(MODEL_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS)
49		async refreshModelId(): Promise<void> {
50			await this.resolveModelId();
51		}
52	
53		private async resolveModelId(): Promise<void> {
54			try {
55				const modelId = await this.opensearchService.findDeployedModelId({
56					modelGroupName: MODEL_GROUP_NAME,
57				});
58	
59				if (modelId && modelId !== this.modelId) {
60					const previousModelId = this.modelId;
61					this.modelId = modelId;
62					this.logger.log(`Model ID updated: ${previousModelId ?? 'none'} -> ${modelId}`);
63				} else if (!modelId) {
64					this.logger.warn(
65						'No deployed model found. Neural search will not work until bootstrap is run.',
66					);
67				}
68			} catch {
69				this.logger.warn('Failed to resolve model ID. OpenSearch may not be available.');
70			}
71		}
72	
73		private async attemptAutoRedeploy(): Promise<void> {
74			try {
75				this.logger.log('Attempting auto-redeploy of neural search model...');
76				const modelId = await this.opensearchService.redeployRegisteredModel({
77					modelGroupName: MODEL_GROUP_NAME,
78				});
79	
80				if (modelId) {
81					this.modelId = modelId;
82					this.logger.log(`Auto-redeploy successful. Model ID: ${modelId}`);
83				} else {
84					this.logger.warn(
85						'No registered model found for auto-redeploy. Run: npm run cli:dev -- bootstrap-neural-search',
86					);
87				}
88			} catch {
89				this.logger.warn('Auto-redeploy failed. OpenSearch may not be available.');
90			}
91		}
92	
93		private getModelId(): string {
94			if (!this.modelId) {
95				throw new Error(
96					'Neural search model not available. Run: npm run cli:dev -- bootstrap-neural-search',
97				);
98			}
99			return this.modelId;
100		}
101	
102		private detectBrand({ text }: { text: string }): string | null {
103			const sorted = [...NEURAL_SEARCH_KNOWN_BRANDS].sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length);
104			for (const brand of sorted) {
105				const escaped = brand.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
106				if (new RegExp(`\\b${escaped}\\b`, 'i').test(text)) {
107					return brand;
108				}
109			}
110			return null;

458		async searchProducts({
459			queryText,

463			queryText: string;
464			size?: number;
465			categoryFilter?: string;
466		}): Promise<IOpensearchProduct[]> {
467			const sanitizedQuery = this.sanitizeQuery({ text: queryText });

603					confidenceScore: Math.min(Math.round(Math.pow(normalizedScore, 2) * 100), 100),
604					...(imageUrl !== null && { style_card_image_url: imageUrl }),
605				};
606			});
607		}

621			categories,
622			size,
623		}: {
624			categories: Record<string, string>[];
625			size?: number;
626		}): Promise<IOpensearchProduct[]> {
627			const effectiveSize = size ?? NEURAL_SEARCH_LIMIT;
628			const perCategory = Math.ceil(effectiveSize / categories.length);
629	
630			const categoryResults = await Promise.all(
631				categories.map((categoryEntry) => {

649						interleaved.push(item);
650					}
651				}
652			}
653	
654			return interleaved.slice(0, effectiveSize);
655		}

672				c.name.toLowerCase().includes(lowerColor),

674

676		}

225		private detectSize({ text }: { text: string }): string | null {
226			const cleanedText = this.stripContractions({
227				text: text
228					.replace(this.MEASUREMENT_UNITS_PATTERN, '')
229					.replace(this.PRICE_STRIP_PATTERN, ''),
230			});
231			const sorted = [...NEURAL_SEARCH_KNOWN_SIZES].sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length);
232			for (const size of sorted) {
233				const escaped = size.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
234				if (new RegExp(`\\b${escaped}\\b`, 'i').test(cleanedText)) {
235					return size;
236				}
237			}
238			return null;
239		}
240

716					: (priceLvlMap['2'] ?? null);
717	
718			const parts: string[] = [
719				...(style.name ? [this.cleanText({ text: style.name })] : []),
720				...(style.product_type ? [this.cleanText({ text: style.product_type })] : []),
721				...(mappedPriceLevel ? [`Price lvl: ${mappedPriceLevel}`] : []),
722				...(style.brand ? [`Brand: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.brand })}`] : []),
723				...(normalizedCondensedFeatures?.length
724					? [this.cleanText({ text: normalizedCondensedFeatures.join(', ') })]
725					: []),
726				...(style.style_type ? [this.cleanText({ text: style.style_type })] : []),
727				...(style.primary_category_name
728					? [`Category: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.primary_category_name })}`]
729					: []),
730				...(style.manufacturer
731					? [`From: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.manufacturer })}`]
732					: []),
733				...(style.sub_category_names?.length
734					? [
735							`Subcategories: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.sub_category_names.join(', ') })}`,
736						]
737					: []),
738				...(style.decoration_method
739					? [`Decoration: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.decoration_method })}`]
740					: []),
741				...(style.features
742					? [`Features: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.features })}`]
743					: []),
744			];
745	
746			const combinedText = parts.filter(Boolean).join('. ');
747	
748			this.logger.debug(
749				`Combined text length for style_id ${style.style_id}: ${combinedText.length} characters`,
750			);
751	
752			return combinedText;
753		}
754	
755		private extractPrice({
756			prices,
757		}: {
758			prices: IOpensearchProduct['default_unit_prices'];
759		}): number | null {
760			if (!prices?.length) return null;
761			const first = prices[0];
762			if (!first) return null;
763			const parsed = parseFloat(first.price);
764			return isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
765		}
766	
767		private cleanText({ text }: { text: string | null | undefined }): string {
768			if (!text) return '';
769			return text
```

60				total: response.total,
61			}),
62		})
63		async getByUseCase(
64			@Param() params: NeuralSearchUseCaseParamDto,
65			@Query() query: NeuralSearchUseCaseLimitDto,
66		): Promise<NeuralSearchUseCaseResponseDto> {

69				...(query.limit !== undefined && { limit: query.limit }),
70			});
71		}
72

75		@ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Returns HTML page with product cards' })
76		@Header('Content-Type', 'text/html')
77		async view(@Query('q') q?: string, @Query('limit') limit?: string): Promise<string> {
78			if (!q) {
79				return this.neuralSearchService.renderSearchPageHtml({ result: null });
80			}
81	
82			const query: NeuralSearchQueryDto = {
83				q,
84				...(limit !== undefined && { limit: Number(limit) }),
85			};
86			const result = await this.neuralSearchService.search({ query });
87	
88			return this.neuralSearchService.renderSearchPageHtml({ result });
89		}
90	}

1	import { Controller, Get, Header, Param, Query } from '@nestjs/common';
2	import { ApiOperation, ApiResponse, ApiTags } from '@nestjs/swagger';
3	
4	import { NeuralSearchService } from '@modules/rest/neural-search/neural-search.service';
5	
6	import { LogExecution } from '@decorators/log-execution.decorator';
7	
8	import { NeuralSearchQueryDto } from '@dto/neural-search/neural-search-query.dto';
9	import { NeuralSearchResponseDto } from '@dto/neural-search/neural-search-response.dto';
10	import {
11		NeuralSearchUseCaseLimitDto,
12		NeuralSearchUseCaseParamDto,
13	} from '@dto/neural-search/neural-search-use-case-query.dto';
14	import { NeuralSearchUseCaseResponseDto } from '@dto/neural-search/neural-search-use-case-response.dto';
15	
16	@ApiTags('Neural Search')
17	@Controller('neural-search')
18	export class NeuralSearchController {
19		constructor(private readonly neuralSearchService: NeuralSearchService) {}
20	
21		@Get()
22		@ApiOperation({
23			summary: 'Search products using natural language via neural embeddings',
24		})
25		@ApiResponse({
26			status: 200,
27			description: 'Returns semantically relevant products',
28			type: NeuralSearchResponseDto,
29		})
30		@ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: 'Invalid query parameters' })
31		@LogExecution({
32			paramFormatter: (params: [NeuralSearchQueryDto]) => ({
33				q: params[0].q,
34				limit: params[0].limit,
35			}),
36			responseFormatter: (response: NeuralSearchResponseDto) => ({
37				total: response.total,
38			}),
39		})
40		async search(@Query() query: NeuralSearchQueryDto): Promise<NeuralSearchResponseDto> {
41			return this.neuralSearchService.search({ query });
42		}
43	
44		@Get('use-case/:useCase')
45		@ApiOperation({ summary: 'Get products by business use-case category' })
46		@ApiResponse({
47			status: 200,
48			description: 'Returns products matching the use-case categories',
49			type: NeuralSearchUseCaseResponseDto,
50		})
51		@ApiResponse({ status: 400, description: 'Invalid use-case' })
52		@LogExecution({
53			paramFormatter: (
54				params: [NeuralSearchUseCaseParamDto, NeuralSearchUseCaseLimitDto],
55			) => ({
56				useCase: params[0].useCase,
57				limit: params[1].limit,
58			}),
59			responseFormatter: (response: NeuralSearchUseCaseResponseDto) => ({
60				total: response.total,
61			}),
62		})
63		async getByUseCase(
64			@Param() params: NeuralSearchUseCaseParamDto,
65			@Query() query: NeuralSearchUseCaseLimitDto,
66		): Promise<NeuralSearchUseCaseResponseDto> {
67			return await this.neuralSearchService.getProductsByUseCase({
68				useCase: params.useCase,
69				...(query.limit !== undefined && { limit: query.limit }),
70			});
71		}
72	
73		@Get('view')
74		@ApiOperation({ summary: 'Visual HTML view of neural search results' })
75		@ApiResponse({ status: 200, description: 'Returns HTML page with product cards' })
76		@Header('Content-Type', 'text/html')
77		async view(@Query('q') q?: string, @Query('limit') limit?: string): Promise<string> {
78			if (!q) {
79				return this.neuralSearchService.renderSearchPageHtml({ result: null });
80			}
81	
82			const query: NeuralSearchQueryDto = {
83				q,
84				...(limit !== undefined && { limit: Number(limit) }),
85			};
86			const result = await this.neuralSearchService.search({ query });
87	
88			return this.neuralSearchService.renderSearchPageHtml({ result });
89		}
90	}
91

1	import { Injectable, Logger, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
2	import { Client } from '@opensearch-project/opensearch';
3	
4	import { EnvConfigService } from '@modules/env-config/env-config.service';
5	
6	import {
7		OPENSEARCH_HOST,
8		OPENSEARCH_PASSWORD,
9		OPENSEARCH_USERNAME,
10	} from '@constants/env-variables.constant';
11	
12	import { LogExecution } from '@decorators/log-execution.decorator';
13	
14	@Injectable()
15	export class OpensearchService implements OnModuleInit {
16		private readonly logger = new Logger(OpensearchService.name);
17		private client: Client;
18		private host: string;
19		private username: string;
20		private password: string;
21	
22		constructor(private readonly configService: EnvConfigService) {}
23	
24		onModuleInit(): void {
25			this.initializeOpensearchClient();
26		}
27	
28		private initializeOpensearchClient(): void {
29			try {
30				this.host = this.configService.get<string>({ key: OPENSEARCH_HOST });
31				this.username = this.configService.get<string>({ key: OPENSEARCH_USERNAME });
32				this.password = this.configService.get<string>({ key: OPENSEARCH_PASSWORD });
33	
34				this.client = new Client({
35					node: this.host,
36					auth: {
37						username: this.username,
38						password: this.password,
39					},
40				});
41	
42				this.logger.log(`OpenSearch client initialized successfully. Host: ${this.host}`);
43			} catch (error: unknown) {
44				this.handleError({ context: 'Failed to initialize OpenSearch client', error });
45				throw error;
46			}
47		}
48	
49		@LogExecution()
50		async healthCheck(): Promise<{ status: string; cluster_name: string }> {
51			try {
52				const response = await this.client.cluster.health();
53				return {
54					status: response.body.status,
55					cluster_name: response.body.cluster_name,
56				};
57			} catch (error: unknown) {
58				this.handleError({ context: 'OpenSearch health check failed', error });
59				throw error;
60			}
61		}
62	
63		getClient(): Client {
64			return this.client;
65		}
66	
67		@LogExecution()
68		async createIndex({
69			indexName,
70			mappings,
71			settings,
72		}: {
73			indexName: string;
74			mappings: Record<string, unknown>;
75			settings: Record<string, unknown>;
76		}): Promise<void> {
77			try {
78				const exists = await this.client.indices.exists({ index: indexName });
79	
80				if (exists.body) {
81					this.logger.log(`Index ${indexName} already exists`);
82					return;
83				}
84	
85				await this.client.indices.create({
86					index: indexName,
87					body: {
88						settings,
89						mappings,
90					},
91				});
92	
93				this.logger.log(`Created OpenSearch index: ${indexName}`);
94			} catch (error: unknown) {
95				this.handleError({ context: `Failed to create index ${indexName}`, error });
96				throw error;
97			}
98		}

226		async deployModel({ modelId }: { modelId: string }): Promise<string> {
227			try {

231				});
232

242		@LogExecution()
243		async getTask({ taskId }: { taskId: string }): Promise<{
244			state: string;
245			model_id?: string;
246		}> {
247			try {
248				const response = await this.client.transport.request({
249					method: 'GET',
250					path: `/_plugins/_ml/tasks/${taskId}`,
251				});
252	
253				const body = response.body as { state: string; model_id?: string };
254				return {
255					state: body.state,
256					...(body.model_id !== undefined && { model_id: body.model_id }),
257				};
258			} catch (error: unknown) {
259				this.handleError({ context: `Failed to get task ${taskId}`, error });
260				throw error;
261			}
262		}
263	
264		@LogExecution()
265		async waitForTask({
266			taskId,
267			intervalMs = 5000,
268			maxAttempts = 60,
269		}: {
270			taskId: string;
271			intervalMs?: number;
272			maxAttempts?: number;
273		}): Promise<{ state: string; model_id?: string }> {
274			for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= maxAttempts; attempt++) {
275				const task = await this.getTask({ taskId });
276				this.logger.log(
277					`Task ${taskId} — attempt ${attempt}/${maxAttempts}, state: ${task.state}`,
278				);
279	
280				if (task.state === 'COMPLETED') {
281					return task;
282				}
283	
284				if (task.state === 'FAILED') {
285					throw new Error(`Task ${taskId} failed`);
286				}
287	
288				await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, intervalMs));
289			}
290	
291			throw new Error(`Task ${taskId} timed out after ${maxAttempts} attempts`);
292		}

566		@LogExecution()
567		async findDeployedModelId({
568			modelGroupName,
569		}: {
570			modelGroupName: string;
571		}): Promise<string | null> {
572			const modelGroupId = await this.searchModelGroupByName({ name: modelGroupName });
573	
574			if (!modelGroupId) {
575				return null;
576			}
577	
578			const models = await this.searchModelsByGroup({ modelGroupId });
579			const deployed = models.find((m) => m.model_state === 'DEPLOYED');
580			return deployed?.model_id ?? null;
581		}
582	
583		@LogExecution()
584		async redeployRegisteredModel({
585			modelGroupName,
586		}: {
587			modelGroupName: string;
588		}): Promise<string | null> {
589			const modelGroupId = await this.searchModelGroupByName({ name: modelGroupName });
590	
591			if (!modelGroupId) {
592				return null;
593			}
594	
595			const models = await this.searchModelsByGroup({ modelGroupId });
596	
597			const deployed = models.find((m) => m.model_state === 'DEPLOYED');
598			if (deployed) {
599				return deployed.model_id;
600			}
601	
602			const redeployable = models.find(
603				(m) =>
604					m.model_state === 'REGISTERED' ||
605					m.model_state === 'UNDEPLOYED' ||
606					m.model_state === 'DEPLOY_FAILED',
607			);
608	
609			if (!redeployable) {
610				return null;
611			}
612	
613			const deployTaskId = await this.deployModel({ modelId: redeployable.model_id });
614			await this.waitForTask({ taskId: deployTaskId, intervalMs: 5000, maxAttempts: 60 });
615	
616			return redeployable.model_id;
617		}
618	
619		private isNotFoundError({ error }: { error: unknown }): boolean {
620			const message = error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error);
621			return message.includes('resource_not_found') || message.includes('404');
622		}
623	
624		private handleError({ context, error }: { context: string; error: unknown }): void {
625			const err = error instanceof Error ? error : new Error(String(error));
626			this.logger.error(`${context}: ${err.message}`, err.stack);
627		}
628	}

100		@LogExecution({ logParams: false, logResponse: false })
101		async bulkIndex({
102			indexName,
103			documents,
104		}: {
105			indexName: string;
106			documents: { _id: string; [key: string]: unknown }[];
107		}): Promise<{ indexed: number; errors: number }> {
108			try {
109				if (documents.length === 0) {
110					return { indexed: 0, errors: 0 };
111				}
112	
113				const body = documents.flatMap((doc) => {
114					const { _id, ...docWithoutId } = doc;
115					return [{ index: { _index: indexName, _id } }, docWithoutId];
116				});
117	
118				const response = await this.client.bulk({ body });
119	
120				const errorItems = response.body.items.filter(
121					(item: { index?: { error?: unknown } }) => item.index?.error,
122				);
123	
124				const errors = errorItems.length;
125				const indexed = documents.length - errors;
126	
127				if (errors > 0) {
128					this.logger.warn(
129						`Bulk index completed with ${errors} errors out of ${documents.length} documents`,
130					);
131	
132					const sampleErrors = errorItems
133						.slice(0, 3)
134						.map((item: { index?: { error?: { type?: string; reason?: string } } }) => ({
135							type: item.index?.error?.type,
136							reason: item.index?.error?.reason,
137						}));
138	
139					this.logger.error(`Sample errors: ${JSON.stringify(sampleErrors, null, 2)}`);
140				}
141	
142				return { indexed, errors };
143			} catch (error: unknown) {
144				this.handleError({ context: 'Bulk index failed', error });
145				throw error;
146			}
147		}

455				this.logger.log(`Deleted search pipeline: ${pipelineId}`);
456			} catch (error: unknown) {
457				if (this.isNotFoundError({ error })) {
458					this.logger.log(`Search pipeline ${pipelineId} does not exist`);
459					return;
460				}
461				this.handleError({
462					context: `Failed to delete search pipeline ${pipelineId}`,
463					error,
464				});
465				throw error;
466			}
467		}
468	
469		@LogExecution()
470		async searchModelsByGroup({
471			modelGroupId,
472		}: {
473			modelGroupId: string;
474		}): Promise<{ model_id: string; model_state: string }[]> {
475			try {
476				const response = await this.client.transport.request({
477					method: 'POST',
478					path: '/_plugins/_ml/models/_search',
479					body: {
480						query: { term: { model_group_id: modelGroupId } },
481						size: 100,
482					},
483				});
484	
485				const hits = (
486					response.body as {
487						hits: { hits: { _id: string; _source: { model_state: string } }[] };
488					}
489				).hits.hits;
490	
491				return hits.map((hit) => ({
492					model_id: hit._id,
493					model_state: hit._source.model_state,
494				}));
495			} catch (error: unknown) {
496				this.handleError({ context: 'Failed to search models', error });
497				throw error;
498			}
499		}

620		async searchProductsByCategories({
621			categories,
622			size,
623		}: {
624			categories: Record<string, string>[];
625			size?: number;
626		}): Promise<IOpensearchProduct[]> {
627			const effectiveSize = size ?? NEURAL_SEARCH_LIMIT;
628			const perCategory = Math.ceil(effectiveSize / categories.length);
629	
630			const categoryResults = await Promise.all(
631				categories.map((categoryEntry) => {
632					const categoryName = Object.keys(categoryEntry)[0] ?? '';
633					const categoryQueryText = Object.values(categoryEntry)[0] ?? '';
634	
635					return this.searchProducts({
636						queryText: categoryQueryText || categoryName,
637						size: perCategory,
638						categoryFilter: categoryName,
639					});
640				}),
641			);
642	
643			const interleaved: IOpensearchProduct[] = [];
644			const maxLen = Math.max(...categoryResults.map((r) => r.length));
645			for (let i = 0; i < maxLen; i++) {
646				for (const results of categoryResults) {
647					const item = results[i];
648					if (item !== undefined) {
649						interleaved.push(item);
650					}
651				}
652			}
653	
654			return interleaved.slice(0, effectiveSize);
655		}
656	
657		private resolveColorImageUrl({
658			product,
659			colorName,
660		}: {
661			product: IOpensearchProduct;
662			colorName: string;
663		}): string | null {
664			if (!product.colors?.length) return null;
665	
666			const lowerColor = colorName.toLowerCase();
667			const match = product.colors.find((c) => c.name.toLowerCase() === lowerColor);
668	
669			if (match?.image_url) return match.image_url;
670	
671			const partialMatch = product.colors.find((c) =>
672				c.name.toLowerCase().includes(lowerColor),
673			);
674	
675			return partialMatch?.image_url ?? null;
676		}
677	
678		private normalizeCondensedFeatures({
679			condensedFeatures,
680		}: {
681			condensedFeatures: unknown;
682		}): string[] | null {
683			if (condensedFeatures === null || condensedFeatures === undefined) {
684				return null;
685			}
686	
687			if (typeof condensedFeatures === 'string') {
688				return [condensedFeatures];
689			}
690	
691			if (Array.isArray(condensedFeatures)) {
692				return condensedFeatures.map(String);
693			}
694	
695			if (typeof condensedFeatures === 'object') {
696				return Object.values(condensedFeatures).flatMap((value: unknown) =>
697					Array.isArray(value) ? value.map(String) : [String(value)],
698				);
699			}

704		private buildCombinedTextForEmbedding({ style }: { style: IStylesMvBase }): string {
705			const normalizedCondensedFeatures = this.normalizeCondensedFeatures({
706				condensedFeatures: style.condensed_features,
707			});
708			const priceLvlMap: Record<string, string> = {
709				1: 'Budget',
710				2: 'Mid-range',
711				3: 'Premium',
712			};
713			const mappedPriceLevel: string | null =
714				style.price_level && priceLvlMap[String(style.price_level)]
715					? (priceLvlMap[String(style.price_level)] ?? null)
716					: (priceLvlMap['2'] ?? null);
717	
718			const parts: string[] = [
719				...(style.name ? [this.cleanText({ text: style.name })] : []),
720				...(style.product_type ? [this.cleanText({ text: style.product_type })] : []),
721				...(mappedPriceLevel ? [`Price lvl: ${mappedPriceLevel}`] : []),
722				...(style.brand ? [`Brand: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.brand })}`] : []),
723				...(normalizedCondensedFeatures?.length
724					? [this.cleanText({ text: normalizedCondensedFeatures.join(', ') })]
725					: []),
726				...(style.style_type ? [this.cleanText({ text: style.style_type })] : []),
727				...(style.primary_category_name
728					? [`Category: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.primary_category_name })}`]
729					: []),
730				...(style.manufacturer
731					? [`From: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.manufacturer })}`]
732					: []),
733				...(style.sub_category_names?.length
734					? [
735							`Subcategories: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.sub_category_names.join(', ') })}`,
736						]
737					: []),
738				...(style.decoration_method
739					? [`Decoration: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.decoration_method })}`]
740					: []),
741				...(style.features
742					? [`Features: ${this.cleanText({ text: style.features })}`]
743					: []),
744			];
745	
746			const combinedText = parts.filter(Boolean).join('. ');
747	
748			this.logger.debug(
749				`Combined text length for style_id ${style.style_id}: ${combinedText.length} characters`,
750			);
751	
752			return combinedText;
753		}

755		private extractPrice({
756			prices,
757		}: {
758			prices: IOpensearchProduct['default_unit_prices'];
759		}): number | null {
760			if (!prices?.length) return null;
761			const first = prices[0];
762			if (!first) return null;
763			const parsed = parseFloat(first.price);
764			return isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
765		}
766	
767		private cleanText({ text }: { text: string | null | undefined }): string {
768			if (!text) return '';
769			return text
770				.replace(/\r\n|\r|\n/g, ' ')
771				.replace(/[*#]+\s?/g, '')
772				.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '')
773				.replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ')
774				.trim();
775		}

200		private detectPriceRange({
201			text,
202		}: {
203			text: string;

205			for (const { pattern, extract } of this.PRICE_RANGE_PATTERNS) {

215			let result = text;

217				result = result.replace(new RegExp(`\\b${contraction}\\b`, 'gi'), '');
218			}
219			return result;

228					.replace(this.MEASUREMENT_UNITS_PATTERN, '')
229					.replace(this.PRICE_STRIP_PATTERN, ''),
230			});
231			const sorted = [...NEURAL_SEARCH_KNOWN_SIZES].sort((a, b) => b.length - a.length);
232			for (const size of sorted) {
233				const escaped = size.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g, '\\$&');
234				if (new RegExp(`\\b${escaped}\\b`, 'i').test(cleanedText)) {
235					return size;
236				}
237			}
238			return null;
239		}
240	
241		private readonly GENDER_PRODUCT_TYPE_MAP: {
242			pattern: RegExp;
243			productTypes: string[];
244		}[] = [
245			{
246				pattern: /\b(women|woman|womens|women's|female|ladies|lady|girls|girl's)\b/i,
247				productTypes: ["Women's"],
248			},
249			{
250				pattern: /\b(men|man|mens|men's|male|guys|guy)\b/i,
251				productTypes: ['Unisex'],
252			},
253			{
254				pattern: /\b(youth|kids|kid|children|child|toddler|infant|baby|juvenile)\b/i,
255				productTypes: ['Youth'],
256			},
257		];
258	
259		private detectGenderProductTypes({ text }: { text: string }): string[] | null {
260			const productTypes: string[] = [];
261	
262			for (const { pattern, productTypes: types } of this.GENDER_PRODUCT_TYPE_MAP) {
263				if (pattern.test(text)) {
264					productTypes.push(...types);
265				}
266			}
267	
268			return productTypes.length > 0 ? productTypes : null;
269		}

755		private extractPrice({
756			prices,
757		}: {
758			prices: IOpensearchProduct['default_unit_prices'];
759		}): number | null {
760			if (!prices?.length) return null;
761			const first = prices[0];
762			if (!first) return null;
763			const parsed = parseFloat(first.price);
764			return isNaN(parsed) ? null : parsed;
765		}
766	
767		private cleanText({ text }: { text: string | null | undefined }): string {
768			if (!text) return '';
769			return text
770				.replace(/\r\n|\r|\n/g, ' ')
771				.replace(/[*#]+\s?/g, '')
772				.replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '')
773				.replace(/\s{2,}/g, ' ')
774				.trim();
775		}

272		public async getRelatedProductIds({
273			productId,
274		}: {
275			productId: number;
276		}): Promise<number[]> {
277			try {
278				const objectIdKey = `${productId}-${ALGOLIA_OBJECT_ID_SUFFIXES[0]}`;
279				const cacheKey = `ids:${this.generateAlgoliaRelatedProductKey({ productId: objectIdKey })}`;
280				const cachedIds = await this.cacheService.get<number[]>({
281					namespace: Namespaces.RELATED_ITEMS_MODEL,
282					key: cacheKey,
283				});
284	
285				if (cachedIds) {
286					this.logger.debug('Cache hit for related product IDs', {
287						productId,
288						objectIdKey,
289					});
290					await this.metricsService.incrementDailyCounter({
291						metricType: dailyTrackingKeys.CACHE_REQUESTS,
292					});
293					return cachedIds;
294				}
295	
296				this.logger.debug('Cache miss for related product IDs', { productId, objectIdKey });
297	
298				const results = await this.algoliaClientRecommend.getRecommendations({
299					requests: [
300						{
301							indexName: this.indexName,
302							model: 'related-products',
303							threshold: 0,
304							objectID: objectIdKey,
305						},
306					],
307				});
308	
309				const recommendationsHits = results.results[0]?.hits ?? [];
310				const styleIds = recommendationsHits
311					.map((hit) => {
312						const productIdValue = (hit as unknown as AlgoliaRelatedProductHit).product_id;
313						return typeof productIdValue === 'string'
314							? Number(productIdValue)
315							: productIdValue;
316					})
317					.filter((id): id is number => !isNaN(id));
318	
319				await this.metricsService.incrementDailyCounter({
320					metricType: dailyTrackingKeys.ALGOLIA_REQUESTS,
321				});

import { BadRequestException, Injectable } from '@nestjs/common';

import { OpensearchNeuralService } from '@modules/opensearch/opensearch-neural.service';

import { NEURAL_SEARCH_TAG_CATEGORY_NAMES } from '@constants/neural-search.constant';

import { LogExecution } from '@decorators/log-execution.decorator';

import { NeuralSearchQueryDto } from '@dto/neural-search/neural-search-query.dto';
import { NeuralSearchResponseDto } from '@dto/neural-search/neural-search-response.dto';
import { NeuralSearchUseCaseResponseDto } from '@dto/neural-search/neural-search-use-case-response.dto';

@Injectable()
export class NeuralSearchService {
	constructor(private readonly opensearchNeuralService: OpensearchNeuralService) {}

	@LogExecution({
		paramFormatter: (params: [NeuralSearchQueryDto]) => ({
			q: params[0].q,
			limit: params[0].limit,
		}),
		responseFormatter: (response: NeuralSearchResponseDto) => ({
			total: response.total,
		}),
	})
	async search({
		query,
	}: {
		query: NeuralSearchQueryDto;
	}): Promise<NeuralSearchResponseDto> {
		const products = await this.opensearchNeuralService.searchProducts({
			queryText: query.q,
			...(query.limit !== undefined && { size: query.limit }),
		});

		return {
			query: query.q,
			total: products.length,
			products,
		};
	}

	@LogExecution({
		paramFormatter: (params: [{ useCase: string; limit?: number }]) => ({
			useCase: params[0].useCase,
			limit: params[0].limit,
		}),
		responseFormatter: (response: NeuralSearchUseCaseResponseDto) => ({
			total: response.total,
		}),
	})
	async getProductsByUseCase({
		useCase,
		limit,
	}: {
		useCase: string;
		limit?: number;
	}): Promise<NeuralSearchUseCaseResponseDto> {
		const categories = NEURAL_SEARCH_TAG_CATEGORY_NAMES[useCase];

		if (!categories) {
			const validUseCases = Object.keys(NEURAL_SEARCH_TAG_CATEGORY_NAMES).join(', ');
			throw new BadRequestException(
				`Invalid use-case "${useCase}". Valid options: ${validUseCases}`,
			);
		}

		const products = await this.opensearchNeuralService.searchProductsByCategories({
			categories,
			...(limit !== undefined && { size: limit }),
		});

		return {
			useCase,
			total: products.length,
			products,
		};
	}

	renderSearchPageHtml({ result }: { result: NeuralSearchResponseDto | null }): string {

import { Injectable, Logger, OnModuleInit } from '@nestjs/common';
import { Interval } from '@nestjs/schedule';

import { IStylesMvBase } from '@modules/db/styles-mv/types/styles-mv.base.interface';
import { EnvConfigService } from '@modules/env-config/env-config.service';
import { OpensearchService } from '@modules/opensearch/opensearch.service';

import {
	NEURAL_SEARCH_LIMIT,
	NEURAL_SEARCH_MAX_QUERY_LENGTH,
} from '@constants/common.constant';
import { OPENSEARCH_INDEX_NAME } from '@constants/env-variables.constant';
import {
	NEURAL_SEARCH_CONTRACTIONS,
	NEURAL_SEARCH_KNOWN_BRANDS,
	NEURAL_SEARCH_KNOWN_COLORS,
	NEURAL_SEARCH_KNOWN_SIZES,
} from '@constants/neural-search.constant';

import { IOpensearchProduct } from '@interfaces/opensearch-product.interface';

import { LogExecution } from '@decorators/log-execution.decorator';

const MODEL_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000;

const MODEL_GROUP_NAME = 'neural-search-model-group';

@Injectable()
export class OpensearchNeuralService implements OnModuleInit {
	private readonly logger = new Logger(OpensearchNeuralService.name);
	private indexName: string;
	private modelId: string | null = null;

	constructor(
		private readonly opensearchService: OpensearchService,
		private readonly configService: EnvConfigService,
	) {}

	async onModuleInit(): Promise<void> {
		this.indexName = this.configService.get<string>({ key: OPENSEARCH_INDEX_NAME });
		await this.resolveModelId();

		if (!this.modelId) {
			await this.attemptAutoRedeploy();
		}
	}

	@Interval(MODEL_REFRESH_INTERVAL_MS)
	async refreshModelId(): Promise<void> {
		await this.resolveModelId();
	}

	private async resolveModelId(): Promise<void> {
		try {
			const modelId = await this.opensearchService.findDeployedModelId({
				modelGroupName: MODEL_GROUP_NAME,
			});

			if (modelId && modelId !== this.modelId) {
				const previousModelId = this.modelId;
				this.modelId = modelId;
				this.logger.log(`Model ID updated: ${previousModelId ?? 'none'} -> ${modelId}`);
			} else if (!modelId) {
				this.logger.warn(
					'No deployed model found. Neural search will not work until bootstrap is run.',
				);
			}
		} catch {
			this.logger.warn('Failed to resolve model ID. OpenSearch may not be available.');
		}
	}

	private async attemptAutoRedeploy(): Promise<void> {
		try {
			this.logger.log('Attempting auto-redeploy of neural search model...');
			const modelId = await this.opensearchService.redeployRegisteredModel({
				modelGroupName: MODEL_GROUP_NAME,
			});

			if (modelId) {

# Conversational Search: Implementation Plan (Ollama + Llama 3.2 3B)

## Initial Prompt

> We have opensearch and neural search. We would like to add LLM between customers prompt and vector search. Purpose of this model will be to analyze customers prompt, find signals, intent, use-case, gender, etc. — all necessary info to recommend better products. Model should ask 1-3 additional questions to clarify if initial prompt was not full enough. We would like to have self-hosted free small model, like Llama 3B params.

## Key Reference Files

- `src/modules/opensearch/opensearch-neural.service.ts` — existing search pipeline (hybrid search, query enrichment, brand/gender/price detection)
- `src/modules/opensearch/opensearch.service.ts` — OpenSearch client methods
- `src/modules/rest/neural-search/` — existing REST endpoints (GET `/api/v1/neural-search`)
- `src/modules/rest/open-ai/open-ai.service.ts` — pattern for OpenAI SDK usage (reusable for Ollama)
- `src/modules/cache/cache.service.ts` — Redis cache for conversation state
- `src/modules/cli/commands/bootstrap-neural-search.command.ts` — CLI bootstrap pattern
- `src/dto/neural-search/` — existing query/response DTOs
- `src/constants/neural-search-brands.constant.ts` — 284 known brands list

## Decisions

| Question | Decision |
|---|---|
| LLM provider | Ollama (self-hosted, free) — OpenAI-compatible API |
| Model | Llama 3.2 3B (`llama3.2:3b`) |
| Multi-turn | Optional — only when LLM deems prompt too vague |
| Integration | Enhanced query text passed to existing `searchProducts()` |
| Existing GET endpoint | Untouched — new POST endpoints added alongside |
| Conversation state | Redis via existing `CacheService` (10min TTL) |
| Fallback | Direct search if Ollama unavailable |

---

## Context

The neural search currently takes raw user queries, applies regex-based enrichment (gender/price/brand detection, synonym normalization), and runs hybrid search (keyword + vector) against OpenSearch. We add a self-hosted LLM (Llama 3.2 3B via Ollama) between user prompt and search to better understand intent, use-case, gender, style, budget, and other signals. The LLM optionally asks 1-3 clarifying questions only when the initial prompt is too vague.

**Existing GET `/api/v1/neural-search` stays untouched.** New POST-based conversation endpoints added alongside it.

---

## New Flow

(semantic) product search powered by OpenSearch ML. Products are indexed with combined embedding text built from name, product type, brand, features, materials, sizes, and price level. At query time, user input is enriched with brand/color/size/gender/price signals before being sent as a hybrid k-NN + keyword query against the OpenSearch index. Results are semantically ranked rather than keyword-matched, improving relevance for natural language queries like \"soft summer t-shirts for outdoor events\".\n\nThree endpoints are exposed: a JSON search endpoint, an HTML debug view for manual testing, and a use-case endpoint that returns curated product sets for 8 predefined business contexts (Onboarding, Trade Shows, Gifting, etc.). > DEVELOPER

code review from claude code in github
1. SECURITY: XSS in renderSearchPageHtml (neural-search.service.ts)
User-controlled and DB-sourced data interpolated directly into HTML without escaping:

value="[queryValue]" — URL ?q= param injected into attribute. Input "><script>alert(1)</script> executes.
<strong>[result.query]</strong> — same user input reflected unescaped.
[p.name] and brand fields — DB values from OpenSearch. A product name containing </div><script> executes.
Add an escapeHtml helper (&, <, >, ", ') before any interpolation.
2. Missing @LogExecution on view endpoint (neural-search.controller.ts:73)
Per CLAUDE.md: required on all public route handlers.

3. Raw status codes in @ApiResponse (neural-search.controller.ts)
Uses status: 200, status: 400. CLAUDE.md requires HttpStatus enum from @nestjs/common.

4. Missing @ApiResponse for 500 on all three controller routes
CLAUDE.md: one @ApiResponse per possible HTTP status. All routes missing the 500 response.

5. Missing @ApiParam for useCase route param (neural-search.controller.ts)
@Param() params: NeuralSearchUseCaseParamDto present but no @ApiParam decorator.

6. Unvalidated limit in view endpoint (neural-search.controller.ts:77)
@Query('limit') limit?: string passed as Number(limit) with no validation. Number('abc') = NaN silently propagates. Use DTO validation or an isNaN guard.

7. Price level fallback bug (opensearch-neural.service.ts — buildCombinedTextForEmbedding)
Products with price_level = null and unmapped levels both fall through to 'Mid-range' as the default. All products without a price level are incorrectly labeled mid-range in embedding text, degrading vector search quality for price-sensitive queries. Fallback for missing/unknown level should be null.

8. @CronJobExecution on manual CLI commands
CLAUDE.md: use @CronJobExecution for scheduled cron jobs, @LogExecution for manual commands. bootstrap-neural-search, redeploy-neural-search, setup-neural-search are manual provisioning commands not listed as cron_job in ktool.yaml. Should use @LogExecution.

lets delete view endpoint at all and html for it

Push running (pre-push hook). Here's what was fixed:

| # | Issue | Fix |
|---|-------|-----|
| 1 | XSS in `renderSearchPageHtml` | Deleted entire `view` endpoint + `renderSearchPageHtml` method (per your request) |
| 2 | Missing `@LogExecution` on `view` | Moot — endpoint deleted |
| 3 | Raw status codes (`200`, `400`) | Replaced with `HttpStatus.OK`, `HttpStatus.BAD_REQUEST`, `HttpStatus.INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR` |
| 4 | Missing `@ApiResponse` 500 on all routes | Added to both `search` and `getByUseCase` |
| 5 | Missing `@ApiParam` on `useCase` route | Added `@ApiParam({ name: 'useCase', type: String, description: '...' })` |
| 6 | Unvalidated `limit` in `view` | Moot — endpoint deleted |
| 7 | Price level fallback bug | Changed `priceLvlMap['2'] ?? null` → `null` for missing/unknown level |
| 8 | `@CronJobExecution` on manual CLIs | Replaced with `@LogExecution()` in all 3: `bootstrap`, `redeploy`, `setup` |

Tests updated accordingly: removed all `view`/`renderSearchPageHtml` tests, updated 2 price-level tests to assert `null` behavior.

> AGENT

Port `9009`, prefix `/api/v1`:

```bash
# Keyword search
curl "http://localhost:9009/api/v1/neural-search?q=cotton+t-shirt&limit=5"

# With limit
curl "http://localhost:9009/api/v1/neural-search?q=polo+shirt+for+office&limit=10"

# Use-case endpoint (valid values: Onboarding, Trade Shows, Events, Gifting, Uniforming)
curl "http://localhost:9009/api/v1/neural-search/use-case/Onboarding"
curl "http://localhost:9009/api/v1/neural-search/use-case/Gifting"

# Pretty-print with jq
curl -s "http://localhost:9009/api/v1/neural-search?q=backpack&limit=3" | jq .
```

If you get `"Neural search model is not deployed"` or empty results, the model hasn't been bootstrapped yet:

```bash
npm run cli:dev bootstrap-neural-search
```

108			try {
109				if (documents.length === 0) {
110					return { indexed: 0, errors: 0 };
111				}
112	
113				const body = documents.flatMap((doc) => {
114					const { _id, ...docWithoutId } = doc;
115					return [{ index: { _index: indexName, _id } }, docWithoutId];
116				});
117	
118				const response = await this.client.bulk({ body });
119	
120				const errorItems = response.body.items.filter(
121					(item: { index?: { error?: unknown } }) => item.index?.error,
122				);
123	
124				const errors = errorItems.length;
125				const indexed = documents.length - errors;
126	
127				if (errors > 0) {
128					this.logger.warn(
129						`Bulk index completed with ${errors} errors out of ${documents.length} documents`,
130					);
131	
132					const sampleErrors = errorItems
133						.slice(0, 3)
134						.map((item: { index?: { error?: { type?: string; reason?: string } } }) => ({
135							type: item.index?.error?.type,
136							reason: item.index?.error?.reason,
137						}));
138	
139					this.logger.error(`Sample errors: ${JSON.stringify(sampleErrors, null, 2)}`);
140				}
141	
142				return { indexed, errors };
143			} catch (error: unknown) {
144				this.handleError({ context: 'Bulk index failed', error });
145				throw error;
146			}
147		}
148	
149		@LogExecution()
150		async configureClusterMLSettings(): Promise<void> {
151			try {
152				await this.client.cluster.putSettings({
153					body: {
154						persistent: {
155							'plugins.ml_commons.only_run_on_ml_node': false,
156							'plugins.ml_commons.model_access_control_enabled': true,
157							'plugins.ml_commons.native_memory_threshold': 99,
158						},
159					},
160				});

> AGENT

The issue: both specs create an index with `default_pipeline: 'products-neural-pipeline'`, but that pipeline doesn't exist in the test OpenSearch. Bulk indexing fails for every document → `indexed: 0`.

Fix: create a no-op pipeline before tests in both specs.

4	import { ConfigFactory } from '@nestjs/config';

9	import { isProdEnv, isStagingEnv, isTestEnv } from '@utils/common.util';
10	
11	export const getEnvFile = (): string => {
12		if (isTestEnv()) return '.env.test';
13	
14		return '.env';
15	};
16	
17	export const getEnvVariables = (): Record<string, string | number | undefined> => ({
18		PORT: process.env['PORT'],
19		NODE_ENV: process.env['NODE_ENV'],
20		GLOBAL_PREFIX: process.env['GLOBAL_PREFIX'],

25		REDIS_URL: process.env['REDIS_URL'],
26		REDIS_READ_URL: process.env['REDIS_READ_URL'],
27		ALG_APP_ID: process.env['ALG_APP_ID'],
28		ALG_API_KEY: process.env['ALG_API_KEY'],
29		ALG_INDEX_NAME: process.env['ALG_INDEX_NAME'],
30		OPENSEARCH_HOST: process.env['OPENSEARCH_HOST'],
31		OPENSEARCH_USERNAME: process.env['OPENSEARCH_USERNAME'],
32		OPENSEARCH_PASSWORD: process.env['OPENSEARCH_PASSWORD'],
33		OPENSEARCH_INDEX_NAME: process.env['OPENSEARCH_INDEX_NAME'],
34		OPENSEARCH_NEURAL_CACHE_TTL: process.env['OPENSEARCH_NEURAL_CACHE_TTL'],
35		OPENSEARCH_DASHBOARDS_URL: process.env['OPENSEARCH_DASHBOARDS_URL'],
36		NEWRELIC_KEY: process.env['NEWRELIC_KEY'],
37		AWS_REGION: process.env['AWS_REGION'],
38		ROLLBAR_ACCESS_TOKEN: process.env['ROLLBAR_ACCESS_TOKEN'],
39		OPENAI_API_KEY: process.env['OPENAI_API_KEY'],
40		MMS_INTERNAL_BASE_URL: process.env['MMS_INTERNAL_BASE_URL'],
41		ARTILLERY_KEY: process.env['ARTILLERY_KEY'],
42		RELATED_PRODUCTS_CACHE_TTL: process.env['RELATED_PRODUCTS_CACHE_TTL'],
43		CI_BASE_URL: process.env['CI_BASE_URL'],
44		DATABASE_URL_READ_REPLICA: process.env['DATABASE_URL_READ_REPLICA'],
45		DATABASE_URL: process.env['DATABASE_URL'],
46		MMS_IMAGES_LAMBDA_HOST: process.env['MMS_IMAGES_LAMBDA_HOST'],
47		ALG_FBT_INDEX_NAME: process.env['ALG_FBT_INDEX_NAME'],
48		EXECUTION_TIME_THRESHOLD_MS: process.env['EXECUTION_TIME_THRESHOLD_MS'],
49		CURATED_ADDONS_CACHE_TTL: process.env['CURATED_ADDONS_CACHE_TTL'],
50		BIGQUERY_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY: process.env['BIGQUERY_SERVICE_ACCOUNT_KEY'],
51	});
52	
53	export const loadEnvVariables = (): ConfigFactory[] => {
54		if (isStagingEnv() || isProdEnv()) {
55			validate({ envConfig: getEnvVariables() });
56			return [getEnvVariables];
57		}
58	
59		const envFilePath = path.resolve(process.cwd(), getEnvFile());
60	
61		if (!fs.existsSync(envFilePath)) {
62			return [() => ({})];
63		}
64	
65		const envConfig = dotenv.parse(fs.readFileSync(envFilePath));
66	
67		validate({ envConfig });
68	
69		return [() => envConfig];
70	};

> AGENT

Root cause: `loadEnvVariables` reads `.env.test` via `dotenv.parse` and returns file values directly — ignoring `process.env`. CI sets `OPENSEARCH_HOST=http://127.0.0.1:9200` but the file has port 9201, and the override is silently ignored.

Fix: let `process.env` take precedence over file values.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01QR7H5spW4AoThajfHgHNwT
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/4thwithme/Documents/repo/recommendations-service/src/utils/get-env-file.util.ts",
  "old_string": "\tconst envConfig = dotenv.parse(fs.readFileSync(envFilePath));\n\n\tvalidate({ envConfig });\n\n\treturn [() => envConfig];",
  "new_string": "\tconst fileConfig = dotenv.parse(fs.readFileSync(envFilePath));\n\n\tconst envConfig: Record<string, string | undefined> = { ...fileConfig };\n\tfor (const key of Object.keys(fileConfig)) {\n\t\tif (process.env[key] !== undefined) {\n\t\t\tenvConfig[key] = process.env[key];\n\t\t}\n\t}\n\n\tvalidate({ envConfig });\n\n\treturn [() => envConfig];"
}
```

> AGENT

Both fixes are live. Summary of what was pushed:

**`6fba64b`** — Bulk index e2e tests: create a no-op ingest pipeline before each test so documents don't fail due to missing `products-neural-pipeline`

**`7144ba3`** — `loadEnvVariables` now lets `process.env` override `.env` file values. Previously it read the file directly and ignored CI's `OPENSEARCH_HOST=http://127.0.0.1:9200`, connecting to port 9201 (the local dev docker port) instead. All the `ECONNREFUSED 127.0.0.1:9201` failures and the resulting 88.49% branch coverage drop should be resolved.

> DEVELOPER

what was the problem?

> AGENT

Two separate problems:

**1. Wrong port in CI (`ECONNREFUSED 127.0.0.1:9201`)**

`loadEnvVariables` reads `.env.test` via `dotenv.parse` and returns the file content directly — it never looks at `process.env`. So even though CircleCI sets `OPENSEARCH_HOST=http://127.0.0.1:9200`, that env var was completely ignored. The file value (`localhost:9201`, the local docker port mapping) always won. OpenSearch in CI runs on port 9200 with no remapping, so every connection failed.

**2. Missing pipeline during bulk index (`indexed: 0`)**

`createProductsIndex` sets `default_pipeline: 'products-neural-pipeline'` on the index. When documents are bulk-indexed, OpenSearch tries to run that pipeline. In the test environment the pipeline was never created, so every document failed with a pipeline-not-found error, resulting in `indexed = 0`.

> DEVELOPER

go push it

470		async searchModelsByGroup({
471			modelGroupId,
472		}: {
473			modelGroupId: string;
474		}): Promise<{ model_id: string; model_state: string }[]> {
475			try {
476				const response = await this.client.transport.request({
477					method: 'POST',
478					path: '/_plugins/_ml/models/_search',
479					body: {
480						query: { term: { model_group_id: modelGroupId } },
481						size: 100,
482					},
483				});
484	
485				const hits = (
486					response.body as {
487						hits: { hits: { _id: string; _source: { model_state: string } }[] };
488					}
489				).hits.hits;
490	
491				return hits.map((hit) => ({
492					model_id: hit._id,
493					model_state: hit._source.model_state,
494				}));
495			} catch (error: unknown) {

499		}
